| Zambia | A [Postal and Courier Services Bill 2026](https://techafricanews.com/2026/07/23/zambia-moves-to-modernize-postal-and-courier-sector-with-new-bill/) went to public written submissions, replacing a 2009 Act that also underwrites postal banking and financial services. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Submissions closed on 27 July 2026 ([bill](https://techafricanews.com/2026/07/23/zambia-moves-to-modernize-postal-and-courier-sector-with-new-bill/), [submissions](https://cajnewsafrica.com/2026/07/24/zambia-expedites-modernisation-of-postal-courier-legislation/)).<br><br>The 2009 Act underwrites postal banking and financial services, so its replacement governs the postal service's agency-banking role — which makes a postal bill a payments instrument.<br><br>Submissions closed one working day after the consultations ended, and it is the only open public call for written comment on a domestic instrument the base holds for the whole window ([consultation](https://cajnewsafrica.com/2026/07/24/zambia-expedites-modernisation-of-postal-courier-legislation/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Zimbabwe | Cabinet directed drafting of a [statutory instrument on e-hailing services](https://cite.org.zw/zimbabwe-moves-to-regulate-e-hailing-services-amid-revenue-concerns/), reported to Parliament in August 2026 with no text, consultation or commencement date published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It is a drafting instruction ([direction](https://cite.org.zw/zimbabwe-moves-to-regulate-e-hailing-services-amid-revenue-concerns/)).<br><br>It is the only platform-economy instrument in this ledger, and the revenue motive named in the reporting is what places it here rather than in transport.</details> | Advanced |

## Statistics legislation

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | Oversight of the statistics office was [transferred by presidential decree](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/jo-francais/2025/F2025075.pdf) in November 2025; the governing statute is still the [1994 legislative decree](https://unstats.un.org/unsd/methodology/dataquality/meetings/training-wks-Jul2025/Session2_ALGERIA.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The office's own presentation to an international quality workshop [names the 1994 legislative decree as the governing statistics law](https://unstats.un.org/unsd/methodology/dataquality/meetings/training-wks-Jul2025/Session2_ALGERIA.pdf), with its organisation set by [a 1995 decree whose article 6 makes it responsible for the technical preparation, conduct, processing and analysis of population censuses](https://unstats.un.org/unsd/dnss/docViewer.aspx?docID=2193).<br><br>2025-11-10 - [a presidential decree transferred oversight of the office](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/jo-francais/2025/F2025075.pdf), and [a Council of Ministers sitting the same autumn took its modernisation](https://www.tsa-algerie.dz/conseil-des-ministres-du-dimanche-5-octobre-le-communique-integral/).<br><br>The institution is being rearranged around a statute that is thirty-two years old, and no replacement bill is on the record.</details> | Advanced |
| Benin | [Loi 2022-07 of 27 June 2022 organises and regulates statistical activity](https://instad.bj/images/docs/lois_decrets/LOI%20N%C2%B0%202022-07%20DU%2027%20JUIN%202022%20STATISTIQUE.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Loi 2022-07, adopted on 2 June 2022 and promulgated on 27 June, organises and regulates statistical activity in Benin](https://instad.bj/images/docs/lois_decrets/LOI%20N%C2%B0%202022-07%20DU%2027%20JUIN%202022%20STATISTIQUE.pdf), replacing the earlier regime under which the [third national statistical development strategy was drawn up](https://instad.bj/images/docs/cns/programme-SNDS/SNDS-3%20VF.pdf).<br><br>No implementing decree, statistical council minute or compliance report under the new statute is held.</details> | No change, a standing statute the base holds for the first time |
| Botswana | The [Statistics Act commenced in December 2010](https://statsbots.org.bw/sites/default/files/documents/Statistics%20Act%202009.pdf) and is unamended; a second development strategy launched in its place. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2010-12-01 — [the Act establishes Statistics Botswana and the national statistical system](https://statsbots.org.bw/sites/default/files/documents/Statistics%20Act%202009.pdf), including the ten-yearly census mandate.<br><br>2025-11-24 — [the second strategy for the development of statistics was launched, after more than a year’s preparation](https://www.paris21.org/news/botswana-launches-its-second-nsds).<br><br>No amendment to the Act is on the record and none is announced.</details> | No change, with the movement in strategy rather than law |
| Burundi | Statistics legislation [is a chain of three acts rather than a code](https://amategeko.gov.bi/wp-content/uploads/2021/07/BOB_No5BIS-2021.pdf), the 2021 recast being the most recent link with a machine-readable text. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Loi n 1/08 du 20 mai 2021 recast the 2007 law organising the national statistical system](https://amategeko.gov.bi/wp-content/uploads/2021/07/BOB_No5BIS-2021.pdf), and is the substantive recast in a chain running from the 2007 statute through this one to a further modification of 24 July 2023 that the statistics institute cites as the governing law.<br><br>No gazetted text of the 2023 modification was located, so the operative law can be named and its current wording cannot be read. Nothing moved on the statute in the window.</details> | No change |
| Cameroon | [Loi 2020/010 on statistical activity has been in force since July 2020](https://www.prc.cm/fr/multimedia/documents/8414-loi-n-2020-010-du-20-07-2020-activite-statistique-cm), with its [2021 implementing decree](https://www.afristat.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/02/CM02_Decret-application-loi-statistique.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Loi 2020/010 on statistical activity was enacted on 20 July 2020](https://www.prc.cm/fr/multimedia/documents/8414-loi-n-2020-010-du-20-07-2020-activite-statistique-cm) and [Decret 2021-690 fixed its modalities in December 2021](https://www.afristat.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/02/CM02_Decret-application-loi-statistique.pdf). [The statistics institute has published guidelines for producing statistics from administrative sources under it](https://ins-cameroun.cm/manuel-des-lignes-directrices-pour-la-production-des-statistiques-de-source-administrative/).<br><br>No compliance report or statistical council record under the statute is held.</details> | No change, a standing statute the base holds for the first time |
| Cape Verde | The national statistical system rests on [Lei 48/IX/2019, gazetted on 19 February 2019](https://boe.incv.cv/Bulletins/View/40755). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Lei 48/IX/2019 was gazetted in Boletim Oficial n.o 18 on 19 February 2019 as the governing statute of the national statistical system](https://boe.incv.cv/Bulletins/View/40755).<br><br>The base carries the statute and the statistical development strategy for 2022-2026, and nothing testing either against practice: no independent evaluation of the statistics institute is held, and the ledger carries that absence as a separate open row.</details> | No change, a standing statute the base holds for the first time |
| Central African Republic | The [2001 statistics law](https://unstats.un.org/unsd/dnss/docViewer.aspx?docID=2170) is still the basis, and a [modernised replacement was validated in April 2026](https://www.icasees.org/index.php/actualites/332-icasees-atelier-pour-la-validation-du-projet-de-loi-statistique-en-republique-centrafricaine). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The founding law of 16 July 2001 [established the national statistical system, the statistics institute and the national statistics council, and is still the institute's own cited legal basis](https://unstats.un.org/unsd/dnss/docViewer.aspx?docID=2170).<br><br>2026-04-23 - a workshop with the presidency, the national assembly, ministries, the central bank and civil society [validated a modernised draft law to replace it, carrying new institutional arrangements](https://www.icasees.org/index.php/actualites/332-icasees-atelier-pour-la-validation-du-projet-de-loi-statistique-en-republique-centrafricaine). No adoption or promulgation is recorded since, so a twenty-five-year-old statute still governs.</details> | Advanced |
| Chad | [Loi 026/PR/2019](https://www.inseed.td/index.php/systeme-statistique-national/textes-reglementaires) organises statistical activity, and two instruments were made under it for the [third census](https://portail.td/rgph-3-le-denombrement-principal-prolonge-jusquau-6-aout-2026/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statute makes the statistics institute the central body of the national statistical system with legal personality and the status of a public administrative establishment; four application decrees sit under it ([statute](https://www.inseed.td/index.php/systeme-statistique-national/textes-reglementaires)).<br><br>A joint arrete of 6 May 2026 set the composition, powers and operation of provincial, departmental and sub-prefectoral census commissions in every administrative circumscription ([arrete](https://tchadinfos.com/2026/05/07/le-gouvernement-met-en-place-les-organes-locaux-du-3eme-recensement-general-de-la-population-et-de-lhabitat/)), and a decret of 20 July 2026 extended the main enumeration to 6 August from an original window closing 21 July ([decret](https://portail.td/rgph-3-le-denombrement-principal-prolonge-jusquau-6-aout-2026/)).<br><br>The statute is also what the restrictive microdata access policy is grounded in ([policy](https://anad.inseed.td/index.php/politique-dacc)).</details> | Advanced |
| Comoros | The [2011 statistics act is in force and unamended](https://justice.gouv.km/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/loi-n-11-003au-du-26-mars-2011-portant-organisation-et-reglementation-des-activites-statistiques-aux-comores.pdf), constituting the national statistical system and its three components. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The act, [deliberated and adopted on 26 March 2011, constitutes the national statistical system as the Conseil national de la statistique, the statistics institute and the statistical services placed within ministries and public bodies](https://justice.gouv.km/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/loi-n-11-003au-du-26-mars-2011-portant-organisation-et-reglementation-des-activites-statistiques-aux-comores.pdf). It [gives those bodies scientific independence, subordinates any census or survey to a prior visa from the Council, and sets fines from 500,000 to 4,500,000 Comorian francs for running a survey without one](https://justice.gouv.km/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/loi-n-11-003au-du-26-mars-2011-portant-organisation-et-reglementation-des-activites-statistiques-aux-comores.pdf).<br><br>Nothing amends, replaces or implements it on file, and no implementing decree is held. The capture stops part-way through article 28, so the later articles are outside what the base can be read for.</details> | No change |
| Congo | [Loi 36-2018, gazetted on 18 October 2018, governs official statistics](https://www.sgg.cg/JO/2018/congo-jo-2018-42.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Loi 36-2018 on official statistics was gazetted on 18 October 2018](https://www.sgg.cg/JO/2018/congo-jo-2018-42.pdf), and [the national statistical development strategy for 2022 to 2026 runs under it](https://www.afristat.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/06/SNDS_2022-2026_Congo.pdf).<br><br>No implementing decree, statistical council record or compliance assessment under the statute is held.</details> | No change, a standing statute the base holds for the first time |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The statistics law's [three implementing decrees were adopted in June 2025](https://web.sgg.gouv.ci/publications/conseils-des-ministres/details/3009), covering the survey visa, pricing, and transfer of administrative files. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-06-18 - the Council of Ministers [adopted three decrees implementing the 2020 statute that amended the 2013 law organising the national statistical system: on the award of the statistical visa for surveys and censuses, on the conditions and pricing of statistical products and services, and on communication of administrative files to the system's administrations for statistical exploitation](https://web.sgg.gouv.ci/publications/conseils-des-ministres/details/3009). Their stated purpose is a coherent and transparent governance framework, protection of personal information in inter-agency data transfers, and a pricing grid keeping statistical data accessible.<br><br>The statute's own text is not held - it could not be fetched - so this communique is the most authoritative dated statement of the framework on the base, and no decree text is held either.</details> | Advanced |
| Djibouti | Two decrees and an order in the period [created a specialised statistical corps](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/decret-n2026-075-pr-mefi-portant-creation-du-statut-particulier-des-cadres-de-linstad/) and [approved the institute’s 2026 budget](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/arrete-n2026-041-pr-mefi-portant-approbation-du-projet-de-budget-previsionnel-de-linstitut-de-la-statistique-de-djibouti-instad-pour-lexercice-2026/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-02-25 - the institute's [2026 forecast budget of 485,686,344 FDJ was approved by order, following board adoption on 18 November 2025 and Council of Ministers review on 23 December](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/arrete-n2026-041-pr-mefi-portant-approbation-du-projet-de-budget-previsionnel-de-linstitut-de-la-statistique-de-djibouti-instad-pour-lexercice-2026/).<br><br>2026-05-12 - a decree [created a specialised civil-service corps of statisticians, engineers, senior administrators and technicians, charged with compiling, analysing and projecting socio-demographic, economic and environmental data](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/decret-n2026-075-pr-mefi-portant-creation-du-statut-particulier-des-cadres-de-linstad/).<br><br>2026-06-11 - a government reorganisation decree [assigns the finance ministry responsibility for the national strategy on collecting, processing and disseminating official statistics](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/n2026-105-pre-fixant-les-attributions-des-ministeres/).<br><br>No founding statistics law is held; these are instruments made under a framework the base does not carry.</details> | Advanced |
| DR Congo | Draft ordonnances-lois organising statistical activity were adopted, the country's first legislation on the subject ([adoption](https://beto.cd/ordonnance-loi-statistique-rdc-recensement-habilitation/)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Council of Ministers adopted draft ordonnances-lois organising statistical activity on 28 August 2026, presented as the country's first legislation on the subject and as adapting the legal and institutional framework ([adoption](https://beto.cd/ordonnance-loi-statistique-rdc-recensement-habilitation/)). The texts are drafts adopted by the executive; the base holds no promulgation and no published text.</details> | Advanced |
| Egypt | The governing statute is [the 1960 statistics and census law](https://old.capmas.gov.eg/Pages/StaticPages.aspx?page_id=23); no replacement is on the record. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statistics agency publishes [the 1960 statute under its own heading of the laws regulating statistical work; its article 1 vests censuses and statistics in a technical body constituted by presidential decree](https://old.capmas.gov.eg/Pages/StaticPages.aspx?page_id=23). It is sixty-six years old and the base holds no amendment or replacement.<br><br>2026-02-02 - the machinery under it moved: [the prime minister chaired the high committee for the 2027 population, housing and establishments census, with the agency and six ministers](https://www.cabinet.gov.eg/News/Details/84892).<br><br>A national statistics strategy [had been in preparation for nearly two years with 18 ministries and research bodies plus international partners](https://mped.gov.eg/singlenews?id=5528&lang=en) and is not published.</details> | No change, a first stated position with no earlier account behind it |
| Equatorial Guinea | The census legal basis was set by [Decree 25/2025 and Resolution 006/2025](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/inege_presenta_los_avances_estrategicos_para_el_v_censo_de_poblacion_y_el_i_censo_agropecuario); no general statistics act is held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The base holds no general statistics statute for Equatorial Guinea. What it holds is instrument-by-instrument authority: the [fifth population census and first agricultural census rest on a 2025 decree and resolution, with a scientific committee starting work](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/inege_presenta_los_avances_estrategicos_para_el_v_censo_de_poblacion_y_el_i_censo_agropecuario).<br><br>The institute's own governance moved in July 2026, when its [board adopted a revised staff statute and approved action plan spending above five million](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/index.php/noticias/el_consejo_de_administracion_del_inege_analiza_y_aprueba_decisiones_para_el_fortalecimiento_institucional).</details> | Advanced |
| Eritrea | The statistics office has never operated under an enacted statistics law; a draft proclamation was recorded as pending ([review](https://www.norad.no/contentassets/7f7da719f25e49169fd844b5bb9b6f15/mid-term-review-of-cooperation-between-national-statistics-office-in-eritrea-and-statistics-norway.pdf)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>A mid-term review of statistical capacity support records that the statistical proclamation, the equivalent of a statistics act, had been submitted to government and that the formal issue was still pending ([review](https://www.norad.no/contentassets/7f7da719f25e49169fd844b5bb9b6f15/mid-term-review-of-cooperation-between-national-statistics-office-in-eritrea-and-statistics-norway.pdf)).<br><br>No later record of enactment was located at the probe date of 31 August 2026, so the national statistics office has operated for at least two decades without a statutory mandate.<br><br>This matters for the surveys the base does hold: a population and health survey was carried out without a law defining who owns the data, who may access it or on what terms it is published.</details> | No change |
| Eswatini | The [Statistics Act of 1967](https://eswatinilii.org/akn/sz/act/1967/14/eng@1998-12-01) is the operative statute, fifty-nine years old and unamended on the base, with no replacement Bill held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Act constitutes the Central Statistical Office and carries its collection powers; no amendment, replacement Bill or commencement is held ([Act](https://eswatinilii.org/akn/sz/act/1967/14/eng@1998-12-01)).<br><br>Under it, the ministry and the population fund launched the 2027 Population and Housing Census together with the 2025/2026 Economic Census in November 2025 ([launch](https://times.co.sz/27831/news/ministry-unfpa-launch-population-housing-economic-census/)).<br><br>The 2026 budget speech reports household mapping and listing begun and E130.5 million allocated ([budget speech](https://parliament.gov.sz/media/speeches/budget/2026_27%20FY%20Budget_Speech_2026%20FINAL.pdf)).<br><br>The gap is the point: the largest statistical operation in a decade runs on a statute written before the internet, and nothing on the base sets a date for replacing it.</details> | No change, a statute of 1967 unamended against a census it must now carry |
| Ethiopia | A successor statistics proclamation was approved by the House on 30 June 2026, replacing the 2005 statute ([approval](https://www.fanamc.com/english/ethiopian-lawmakers-approve-carbon-market-tax-administration-statistical-service-proclamations/)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Council of Ministers referred the draft Ethiopian Statistics Proclamation to the House of People's Representatives on 26 May 2026 ([referral](https://www.ena.et/web/eng/w/eng_8922874)), and the House approved it on 30 June 2026, intended to strengthen the statistics service's independence and align the national statistical system with international statistical principles ([approval](https://www.fanamc.com/english/ethiopian-lawmakers-approve-carbon-market-tax-administration-statistical-service-proclamations/)).<br><br>The statute it replaces is Proclamation 442/2005, which established the Central Statistics Authority as an autonomous federal agency with legal personality and defined the national statistical programme ([Proclamation 442/2005](https://www.lawethiopia.com/images/federal_proclamation/proclamations_by_number/442.pdf)).<br><br>No gazette text or proclamation number for the new law had been located at the probe date of 28 August 2026, so what is held is the approval, not the enacted text.</details> | Advanced |
| Gabon | A [bill amending the statistical system law went to the Council of Ministers on 22 May 2026](https://agpgabon.ga/gabon-conseil-des-ministres-vers-la-revision-du-cadre-legal-du-systeme-statistique-national/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The planning minister presented a bill amending Loi 15/2014 of 7 January 2015 instituting and organising the Systeme Statistique National, as modified by Loi 016/2022 of 6 September 2022, to bring it into conformity with the Constitution of 19 December 2024 and make the system's organs fully operational](https://agpgabon.ga/gabon-conseil-des-ministres-vers-la-revision-du-cadre-legal-du-systeme-statistique-national/). [The statistics institute's own statutes were fixed by Decret 0314 of August 2024](https://journal-officiel.ga/21242-0314-pr-mpp-/), and [AFRISTAT ran a data-quality diagnostic mission in 2026](https://www.afristat.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/lla116.pdf).<br><br>It was a bill in Council and not an enacted law at capture. The institutional record is held and no statistical output from the office is.</details> | Advanced |
| Gambia | The [2005 Act](https://www.gbosdata.org/downloads-file/167-statistics-act-2005) is unamended, with [the African Union peer review](https://statafric.au.int/en/news/press-releases/2025-11-17/peer-review-report-national-statistical-system-gambia) urging a modernised law in November 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [Statistics Act 2005](https://www.gbosdata.org/downloads-file/167-statistics-act-2005) is the constitutive statute of the statistics bureau, held here only as an image-only scan with no machine-readable text.<br><br>Both reviews of the system call for its revision. The [national statistical strategy](https://www.gbosdata.org/downloads-file/526-national-strategy-for-the-development-of-stati) found more than a third of officials unaware of the Act it says needs revising, and the [African Union peer review of November 2025](https://statafric.au.int/en/news/press-releases/2025-11-17/peer-review-report-national-statistical-system-gambia) urged mandate reform and a modernised statistics law. No amending bill has been laid.</details> | No change |
| Ghana | The [2019 statute is still the governing statistics law](https://ghalii.org/akn/gh/act/2019/1003) and a [stakeholder engagement opened on a replacement bill in July 2026](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/ghana-statistical-service_ghana-statistics-statsbill26-activity-7487065310030630913-ZlEw). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The founding statute [came into force on 27 September 2019, establishing the statistical service as the central statistics-producing and co-ordinating institution for the national statistical system and repealing the 1985 law](https://ghalii.org/akn/gh/act/2019/1003); the service's own page carries it as the [currently governing framework](https://statsghana.gov.gh/about/statistical-service-act).<br><br>2026-07-26 - the service [opened a stakeholder engagement with ministries, development partners, civil society and academia on a proposed replacement bill](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/ghana-statistical-service_ghana-statistics-statsbill26-activity-7487065310030630913-ZlEw). It is a consultation: no bill is shown as introduced, read or passed, and no draft text is held.<br><br>The held capture of the Act itself stops at section 19 of a longer statute, so its offences and miscellaneous provisions are not on the base.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea-Bissau | The [2007 framework law](https://stat-guinebissau.com/Menu_principal/Pubica%C3%A7%C3%B5es/Regulamento%20e%20Estatuto%20Organico/Legisla%C3%A7%C3%A3o_INE.pdf) still governs, and the census rests on it. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The national statistical system framework law of 2007 is the governing statute and no amendment appears in the base.<br><br>The system it governs is delivering: the [fourth census launched field enumeration in June 2026, the country's first fully digital count since 2009](https://guinea-bissau.unfpa.org/en/news/historic-milestone-guinea-bissau-launches-4th-national-census-map-data-driven-future), after the institute [opened recruitment for about 5,000 census staff](https://ang.gw/recenseamento-geral-da-populacao-ine-gb-lanca-sua-plataforma-oficial-de-recrutamento/) in September 2025.</details> | No change |
| Kenya | The [Statistics Act of 2006](https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/2006/4) is under replacement by a 2026 bill establishing a statistics authority with stated professional independence. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Act of 2006 has governed official statistics for two decades ([act](https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/2006/4)).<br><br>A bill published in April 2026 would replace it, establishing a Kenya Statistics Authority whose guiding principles include professional independence ([bill](https://www.knbs.or.ke/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/The-Statistics-Bill-2026.pdf)).<br><br>Sector statistical obligations continued to be made under other statutes: regulations of November 2025 require statistical returns from petroleum licensees ([regulations](https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/ln/2025/187/eng@2025-11-14)). The bill is published and not enacted.</details> | Advanced |
| Lesotho | A [fourth Statistics Bill is expected to be enacted before the end of 2026](https://sundayexpress.co.ls/?p=36134), replacing a statute from 2001. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2001 — [Act 8 of 2001 establishes the Bureau of Statistics and the national statistical system](http://www.bos.gov.ls/BOS_Act_2001.htm).<br><br>2025-12-12 — [a regional peer review recorded a fourth Statistics Bill in drafting and recommended modernising the 2001 Act](https://statafric.au.int/sites/default/files/2025-12/ef_peerreview_lesotho_vf.pdf).<br><br>2026-06-27 — [bureau officials described the Bill’s content and expected enactment before the end of 2026](https://sundayexpress.co.ls/?p=36134).<br><br>The Bill text is not held.</details> | Advanced |
| Liberia | The [2004 Act](https://lisgis.gov.lr/document/lisgisact.pdf) stands unamended, and was tested in the House in [June 2026](https://www.liberianobserver.com/news/hor-summons-nec-lisgis-over-constitutional-threshold-saga/article_ed8424d6-121e-41d6-83e7-a3319f49f041.html). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [National Statistics and Geo-Information Act](https://lisgis.gov.lr/document/lisgisact.pdf), approved 22 July 2004, creates the statistics office as an autonomous agency, empowers it to demand information from any ministry, bureau or agency, and binds its staff to an oath of confidentiality.<br><br>No amendment moved in the window. What did move is a test of the statutory role: in [June 2026 the House summoned](https://www.liberianobserver.com/news/hor-summons-nec-lisgis-over-constitutional-threshold-saga/article_ed8424d6-121e-41d6-83e7-a3319f49f041.html) the director general and his statisticians to justify the population benchmarks behind a proposed expansion of the chamber from 73 to 87 seats.</details> | No change |
| Libya | [Law 16 of 1963](https://lawsociety.ly/legislation/%d9%82%d8%a7%d9%86%d9%88%d9%86-%d8%b1%d9%82%d9%85-16-%d9%84%d8%b3%d9%86%d8%a9-1963-%d9%85-%d9%81%d9%8a-%d8%b4%d8%a3%d9%86-%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%a5%d8%ad%d8%b5%d8%a7%d8%a1%d8%a7%d8%aa-%d9%88%d8%a7%d9%84/) still governs statistics and censuses, its penalty scale unchanged since enactment. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [statute](https://lawsociety.ly/legislation/%d9%82%d8%a7%d9%86%d9%88%d9%86-%d8%b1%d9%82%d9%85-16-%d9%84%d8%b3%d9%86%d8%a9-1963-%d9%85-%d9%81%d9%8a-%d8%b4%d8%a3%d9%86-%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%a5%d8%ad%d8%b5%d8%a7%d8%a1%d8%a7%d8%aa-%d9%88%d8%a7%d9%84/) creates the central statistics committee and remains the governing law, with penalties still expressed in the currency unit of 1963.<br><br>Nothing has replaced it. The [statistics development strategy that ran to 2023](https://paris21.org/sites/default/files/2018-04/NSDS-Libya-En.pdf) carried no adoption decree the base could locate and has not been renewed, so the sector runs on a sixty-three-year-old law and an expired plan.</details> | No change |
| Madagascar | The [2018 statistics law](https://instat.mg/documents/upload/main/INSTAT_LoiStatistique2018-004_18-04-2018.pdf) remains in force; activity in the window was implementation rather than amendment. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The law of April 2018 repealed the 1967 statistics obligation and established the national statistical system, its council and the statistics office's statutory role ([law](https://instat.mg/documents/upload/main/INSTAT_LoiStatistique2018-004_18-04-2018.pdf)).<br><br>Alignment of public finance statistics to international standards continued under external technical assistance in March 2026 ([alignment](http://www.tresorpublic.mg/?p=38388)).<br><br>A project document for the fourth general population and housing census was presented in May 2026 ([document](https://www.instat.mg/p/presentation-du-document-de-projet-prodoc-du-rgph-4)). The law itself is held to its first title only; its 78 articles are not fully captured.</details> | No change |
| Malawi | An [amendment of April 2026 opened protected tax data to the statistics commissioner](https://malawilii.org/akn/mw/act/2026/6/eng@2026-04-14), above the [2013 Act](https://malawilii.org/akn/mw/act/2013/6/eng@2014-12-31). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The operative law is now held: the [National Statistics Act, chapter 27:01, commenced 19 July 2013 and establishes the National Statistical Office and the national statistical system](https://malawilii.org/akn/mw/act/2013/6/eng@2014-12-31). Nothing amends it.<br><br>What changed came from outside it. 2026-04 - the [Tax Administration (Amendment) Act 2026, Act 6 of 2026, assented 9 April, permits the revenue commissioner general to disclose protected tax data to the Commissioner of National Statistics](https://malawilii.org/akn/mw/act/2026/6/eng@2026-04-14). The Act's own text failed to render at capture, so what safeguards attach to that disclosure is not knowable from the base.<br><br>2026-04 - the statistics commissioner [described the finalised strategic plan as expanding participation from 17 to 35 ministries and agencies](https://mwnation.com/nso-renews-strategic-plan/).</details> | Advanced |
| Mali | [Law 2016-005 of 24 February 2016](https://sgg-mali.ml/JO/2016/mali-jo-2016-12.pdf) governs public statistics; the national statistics council and the master plan run under it. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The text is held from the official journal ([law](https://sgg-mali.ml/JO/2016/mali-jo-2016-12.pdf)).<br><br>The national statistics council held an ordinary session in February 2026 ([council](https://instat-mali.org/fr/actualites-et-evenements/session-ordinaire-du-conseil-national-de-la-statistique-cns-)), and the statistical master plan for 2020-2024 reached the seventeenth technical session of its annual review in August 2026 ([review](https://instat-mali.org/fr/actualites-et-evenements/dix-septieme-session-technique-de-la-revue-annuelle-du-schema-directeur-de-la-statistique)) — a plan whose own period expired two years ago and which has no successor on the record.</details> | No change |
| Mauritius | The [Statistics Act 2000](https://statsmauritius.govmu.org/Documents/About_Us/Legislations/Statistics_Act_2000_All_Amended_Yr11.pdf) is under revision, with the office's independence carried in the 2026-2027 budget annex. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Act has been in force since April 2001 and is held in its consolidated, amended form ([Act](https://statsmauritius.govmu.org/Documents/About_Us/Legislations/Statistics_Act_2000_All_Amended_Yr11.pdf)).<br><br>The 2026-2027 budget annex carries the independence of the statistics office ([annex](https://govmu.org/EN/budget/Documents/2026_27_annexBudget.pdf)), and the Finance Act 2026 amends the statute book around it ([Act](https://mauritiusassembly.govmu.org/mauritiusassembly/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/Act1426.pdf)).<br><br>An observance of standards and codes data module assessed the statistical system in December 2025 ([assessment](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2025/english/1musea2025003-source-pdf.pdf)). The independence provision is announced; no amending text is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Morocco | [Law 046.26](https://www.maroc24.com/fr/202293-chambre-des-conseillers-adoption-a-l-unanimite-de-deux-projets-de-loi-relatifs-au-hcp-et-au-systeme-statistique-national/) creates a national statistical information council, replacing a governing decree of 1968. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The system had been governed by a [1968 royal decree creating a statistics coordination committee](https://unstats.un.org/unsd/dnss/docViewer.aspx?docID=46), which remained the operative law for fifty-eight years.<br><br>The replacement moved quickly: [approved by the government council on 2 July 2026](https://www.linformation.ma/news/politique/le-conseil-de-gouvernement-approuve-un-projet-de-loi-relatif-au-systeme-statistique-national/64631), [adopted unanimously by the second chamber on 13 July](https://www.maroc24.com/fr/202293-chambre-des-conseillers-adoption-a-l-unanimite-de-deux-projets-de-loi-relatifs-au-hcp-et-au-systeme-statistique-national/) with the companion law reforming the planning commission, and [promulgated on 28 July as a 51-article statute splitting the statistical and evaluation functions](https://adala.justice.gov.ma/api/uploads/2026/08/28/%D8%B8%D9%87%DB%8C%D8%B1%20%D8%B4%D8%B1%DB%8C%D9%81%20%D8%B1%D9%82%D9%85%201.26.70%20%D8%A8%D8%AA%D9%86%D9%81%D9%8A%D8%B0%20%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%82%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%88%D9%86%20%D8%B1%D9%82%D9%85%20047.26%20%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%84%D9%82%20%D8%A8%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AF%D9%88%D8%A8%D9%8A%D8%A9-1787927322088.pdf).</details> | Advanced |
| Mozambique | [Law 7/96 remains the operative statute creating the National Statistical System](https://ieda.gov.mz/poema/EST-S1/EST-S1-Lei_7_96_de_5_de_Julho.pdf), unamended, while a personal-data bill bearing on statistical collection [went to Parliament in March 2026](https://intic.gov.mz/proposta-de-lei-de-proteccao-de-dados-pessoais-segue-para-debate-na-assembleia-da-republica/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The founding instrument has not moved: [Law 7/96 of 5 July creates the National Statistical System and the statistics institute's mandate](https://ieda.gov.mz/poema/EST-S1/EST-S1-Lei_7_96_de_5_de_Julho.pdf) and is still operative thirty years on.<br><br>2026-03-03 — the Council of Ministers [approved a Personal Data Protection Bill for submission to Parliament](https://intic.gov.mz/proposta-de-lei-de-proteccao-de-dados-pessoais-segue-para-debate-na-assembleia-da-republica/), bearing on statistical collection, consent and cross-border transfer. It is an approved proposal, not an enacted law.<br><br>2025-08-18 — a ministerial diploma [placed the production and publication of public-finance statistics inside the finance ministry's own internal regulation](https://www.mef.gov.mz/index.php/todas-publicacoes/legislacao/2415-br-157-i-serie-suplemento-2025/file), an institutional mandate rather than a change to the statistical law.</details> | No change, the founding statute stands while two adjacent instruments moved around it |
| Namibia | The [Statistics Act of 2011](https://www.lac.org.na/laws/2011/4777.pdf) constitutes the agency and the national statistics system, and five standards were issued under it in September 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Act provides for the statistics agency, the national statistics system and the spatial data infrastructure ([Act](https://www.lac.org.na/laws/2011/4777.pdf)).<br><br>Five standards were published under its standards mandate in September 2025: statistical quality indicators, statistical metadata, leave no one behind, data and statistics presentation, and data and statistics dissemination ([standards](https://nsa.org.na/nss/?page_id=13885)).<br><br>The agency's own strategic plan runs to 2026/27, alongside a strategy for the development of statistics over the same period.<br><br>The Act is fifteen years old and has not needed amending on this base — which distinguishes it from the data protection statute the same government has been drafting for six.</details> | Advanced |
| Niger | Draft texts instituting the national statistical system were [adopted in Council of Ministers in January 2026](https://www.gouv.ne/index.php/les-communiques-du-gouvernement/1028-conclusions-du-conseil-des-ministres-du-vendredi-23-janvier-2026), replacing a framework from 2004. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [Council of Ministers of 23 January 2026](https://www.gouv.ne/index.php/les-communiques-du-gouvernement/1028-conclusions-du-conseil-des-ministres-du-vendredi-23-janvier-2026) adopted draft texts instituting the fundamental principles of statistical activity and the national statistical system, creating a national statistical development fund, redefining the statistics council and updating the statistics institute's legal status.<br><br>The institute's director had reported at a [congress on 8 October 2025](https://www.lesahel.org/7e-congres-ordinaire-du-syndicat-national-du-personnel-de-linformation-statistique-synpis-le-recensement-general-de-la-population-le-bilan-du-mandat-ecoule-et-le-renouvellement-du-b/) that a committee had drafted the ordinance and submitted it to the government's secretariat general for adoption.<br><br>The instrument in force meanwhile is the [2004 statistics law](https://unstats.un.org/unsd/dnss/docViewer.aspx?docID=2129). No promulgated text of the new ordinance is on file.</details> | Advanced |
| Nigeria | The [Statistics Bill 2025 passed third reading in December 2025](https://naltf.gov.ng/house-of-representatives-moves-to-modernize-nigerias-statistical-system-with-new-bill/) and went to [public hearing in June 2026](https://guardian.ng/featured/reps-begin-review-of-statistics-act-link-reform-to-1tn-economy-drive/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The House of Representatives [passed the bill at third reading on 10 December 2025](https://naltf.gov.ng/house-of-representatives-moves-to-modernize-nigerias-statistical-system-with-new-bill/), to repeal the 2007 Act and redefine the statistics bureau's powers and funding.<br><br>Its committee [held a public hearing on 8 June 2026](https://guardian.ng/featured/reps-begin-review-of-statistics-act-link-reform-to-1tn-economy-drive/), at which the speaker and the statistician-general described the bill as a complete overhaul of the 2007 Act rather than an amendment.<br><br>The [Statistics Act 2007](https://www.nigerianstat.gov.ng/resource/NBS_ACT_2007.pdf) remains operative. No Senate stage and no assent is on record, so the bill is two chambers short of law.</details> | Advanced |
| Rwanda | The [2013 statistical activities law](https://statistics.gov.rw/sites/default/files/documents/2025-05/Official_Gazette_no_Special_of_16.06.2013%20%281%29.pdf) remains the governing statute, and a [draft central bank law](https://www.parliament.gov.rw/fileadmin/user_upload/Parliament/Resources/Bills_Before_Parliament/Draft_Law_governing_BNR.pdf) would add a publication obligation. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statute in force since June 2013 founds the national statistical system and assigns the statistics institute its coordinating role; it is still cited as primary on the institute's own legal page ([law](https://statistics.gov.rw/sites/default/files/documents/2025-05/Official_Gazette_no_Special_of_16.06.2013%20%281%29.pdf)).<br><br>A draft law reforming the central bank, whose relevance the Chamber of Deputies approved on 12 February 2026, would make publication of statistical and financial data and studies an express obligation under its article 69 ([explanatory note](https://www.parliament.gov.rw/fileadmin/user_upload/Parliament/Resources/Bills_Before_Parliament/Draft_Law_governing_BNR.pdf)).<br><br>That is at draft stage. The data governance framework published in November 2025 sits below the statute rather than amending it ([framework](https://statistics.gov.rw/sites/default/files/documents/2025-11/National%20Data%20Governance%20Framework.pdf)).</details> | Advanced |
| Sao Tome and Principe | The [1998 statistics law](https://www.ine.st/Recenseamento/Lei%20n5-98-%20Leis%20de%20Base%20do%20Sistema%20Estat%C3%ADstico%20Nacional_PDF.pdf) defines the national statistical system, creates the statistics institute and sets a regime of statistical secrecy and transgressions. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The law names the national statistics council, the statistics institute, the central bank and the institute's delegated organs as the system's organs, creates the institute in Artigo 7 as the body producing and analysing official statistics, and sets fines in Artigos 11 to 15 ([law](https://www.ine.st/Recenseamento/Lei%20n5-98-%20Leis%20de%20Base%20do%20Sistema%20Estat%C3%ADstico%20Nacional_PDF.pdf)).<br><br>It was passed by the National Assembly on 12 August 1998 and promulgated on 31 August 1998 ([law](https://www.ine.st/Recenseamento/Lei%20n5-98-%20Leis%20de%20Base%20do%20Sistema%20Estat%C3%ADstico%20Nacional_PDF.pdf)). Nothing amending or replacing it is held, so a statistical system now running a census, a household budget survey and an automatic electoral register rests on an instrument written before any of them.</details> | No change |
| Senegal | The [2004 statistics law as amended in 2012](https://www.afristat.org/lois-statistiques-des-etats-membres/) remains the framework, with no revision adopted or tabled in the window. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The regional inventory records the operative chain: [loi 2012-03 of 3 January 2012 modifying and completing the 2004 law on the organisation of statistical activities, 2005 decrees on the national statistics council and on the statistics agency, orders on the council's thematic groups and its membership, and 2020 orders on the statistical visa](https://www.afristat.org/lois-statistiques-des-etats-membres/).<br><br>No later replacement statute appears in the inventory, and no adopted or tabled revision was found inside the window.<br><br>Two limits carry: the base holds a regional inventory rather than the statute itself, and the national archive holds only a scan of the decree transmitting the 2012 bill to the National Assembly rather than the enacted text.</details> | No change |
| Seychelles | The [National Bureau of Statistics Act 2010](https://www.nbs.gov.sc/images/Act14-NBS.pdf) is in force, and the African Union's 2025 peer review recommends replacing it. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Act was assented on 9 July 2010, founding the Bureau and the national statistical system and repealing the 2005 statute ([Act](https://www.nbs.gov.sc/images/Act14-NBS.pdf)).<br><br>The peer review conducted in May 2025 and published in October records the Bureau's professional independence and recommends a new Statistics Bill ([review](https://statafric.au.int/en/news/press-releases/2025-10-17/strengthening-seychelles-national-statistical-system)); nothing held records one being drafted.<br><br>The census the Bureau ran in 2022 was conducted under a separate Census Act, itself revised by the 2023 omnibus amendments ([amendments](https://www.gazette.sc/sites/default/files/2023-11/Act%2022%20-%202023%20-%20Miscellaneous%20Amendments%20Act%202023.pdf)).</details> | No change |
| Sierra Leone | The [Statistics Act 2002](https://www.statistics.sl/images/StatisticsSL/Documents/statistics_and_census_acts.pdf) establishes Statistics Sierra Leone as a body corporate for official statistics, and is unamended in the base. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Act was gazetted on 7 November 2002 as an Act to establish Statistics Sierra Leone for the collection, compilation, analysis and dissemination of official and other statistics ([Act](https://www.statistics.sl/images/StatisticsSL/Documents/statistics_and_census_acts.pdf)).<br><br>No amendment, review or replacement is on record, and the statistical strategies recorded separately run under it rather than change it. A census Act sits alongside it in the same published compilation.</details> | No change |
| Somalia | The [National Statistics Law of 2020](https://nbs.gov.so/about-us/) remains the governing statute, having replaced a 1970 law; its own text is not held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Law No. 24, assented on 24 February 2020, established the national statistics bureau and repealed the Statistics Law No. 35 of 1970 ([bureau](https://nbs.gov.so/about-us/)).<br><br>No direct link to the statute's own text was found; the bureau's institutional statement is the fullest account the base can reach, which is a real limit on what can be said about its provisions.<br><br>Law No. 46 of 2026 adds a vital-statistics mandate alongside it without amending it ([law](https://www.osagsomalia.com/media/documents/bulletins-contents/legislation/2026/sharciga_Dad-dhigista_iyo_Tirakoobka_Xaaladaha_Madaniga.pdf)).</details> | No change |
| South Africa | The Statistics Amendment Act [has been proclaimed for implementation](https://www.gov.za/news/speeches/deputy-minister-nonceba-mhauli-stats-sa-202526-q3-report-portfolio-committee-planning), with [regulations and twenty memoranda of understanding under development](https://www.parliament.gov.za/storage/app/media/Docs/atc/01ls62wgfubry77aimpfb3gfqgvq2vzzwq.pdf) to carry it. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The Statistics Act of 1999 is the standing statute](https://www.gov.za/sites/default/files/gcis_document/201409/a6-99.pdf) and [the Amendment Act was assented in December 2024](https://www.gov.za/sites/default/files/gcis_document/202501/51805-statisticsamendmentact29of2024.pdf).<br><br>2026-03-11 — [the Amendment Act had been proclaimed for implementation and implementation was reported as progressing](https://www.gov.za/news/speeches/deputy-minister-nonceba-mhauli-stats-sa-202526-q3-report-portfolio-committee-planning); the proclamation date itself is not stated in the held account.<br><br>2026-06-23 — [regulations and twenty memoranda of understanding are under development under the amended Act](https://www.parliament.gov.za/storage/app/media/Docs/atc/01ls62wgfubry77aimpfb3gfqgvq2vzzwq.pdf). Those memoranda are the mechanism by which the statistics office would obtain other departments' administrative data, which makes them the operative instrument rather than the Act.<br><br>Nothing states which departments they cover or when they are expected.</details> | Advanced |
| South Sudan | The [National Bureau of Statistics Act 2024](https://nbs.gov.ss/resources/Laws_Policies.php) superseded the 2011 Act, though its text is not published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The bureau's own legal-framework page identifies the 2024 Act, assented in November 2024, as its principal legislation, superseding the 2011 Southern Sudan Centre for Census, Statistics and Evaluation Act ([framework](https://nbs.gov.ss/resources/Laws_Policies.php)).<br><br>The Director General briefed the Vice President's office in September 2025 with the Act as one of two documents discussed alongside a census development tool ([briefing](https://www.eyeradio.org/statistics-bureau-develops-population-data-collection-tool-ahead-of-2026-elections/)).<br><br>The Act's full text is not reachable through the bureau's pages or the justice ministry's laws index, which still lists only the 2011 Act. A statute in force that a citizen cannot read is the standing condition of much of this base.</details> | Advanced |
| Sudan | The [Statistics Act 2003](https://data.unwomen.org/sites/default/files/documents/Publications/Assessment%20of%20Sudan%20National%20Gender%20Statistics%20System.pdf) remains in force and was assessed in 2021 as not providing for recent advances in statistical production. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A joint assessment of the national statistical system, with fieldwork between March and July 2021, states plainly that the Act is outdated, does not adequately provide for recent advances in statistical production and contains no gender-statistics provisions ([assessment](https://data.unwomen.org/sites/default/files/documents/Publications/Assessment%20of%20Sudan%20National%20Gender%20Statistics%20System.pdf)).<br><br>That is the most current authoritative statement of the Act's standing the base holds. No full primary text of the Act itself was locatable, and no amendment or successor bill has been found.</details> | No change |
| Zambia | The 2018 Act [is operating on its own terms](https://www.parliament.gov.zm/sites/default/files/documents/acts/The%20Statistics%20Act%202018.pdf): a [release calendar issued under section 27(1)](https://www.zamstats.gov.zm/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/2026-NSRC-1.12.2025.pdf) and a [Statistician-General appointed under section 11](https://www.zamstats.gov.zm/important-notice-executive-appointment-statistician-general/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Act No. 13 of 2018 establishes the statistics agency and the national statistical system and remains the operative framework](https://www.parliament.gov.zm/sites/default/files/documents/acts/The%20Statistics%20Act%202018.pdf).<br><br>2025-12 — [a national statistical release calendar for 2026 was issued expressly under section 27(1) of the Act](https://www.zamstats.gov.zm/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/2026-NSRC-1.12.2025.pdf), operationalising its release-planning mandate.<br><br>2026-03-24 — [a new Statistician-General was appointed, the statutory chief executive under section 11](https://www.zamstats.gov.zm/important-notice-executive-appointment-statistician-general/).<br><br>Both are the statute working rather than changing, which is the more useful thing to record about a law eight years old: the offices it creates are filled and the duties it imposes are being discharged on schedule.</details> | Advanced |
| Zimbabwe | The [Census and Statistics Act](https://zimlii.org/akn/zw/act/2007/1/eng@2020-09-04) is in force as consolidated in 2020, and the statistics agency said in October 2025 that it is outdated. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Act, consolidated on 4 September 2020, constitutes the statistics agency and carries its census, survey and data-collection powers ([Act](https://zimlii.org/akn/zw/act/2007/1/eng@2020-09-04)).<br><br>On World Statistics Day 2025 the agency said the Act is outdated and does not adequately support the efficient provision of official statistics, naming uneven connectivity in rural areas as its other operational constraint ([statement](https://www.newsday.co.zw/local-news/article/200047510/outdated-laws-hinder-delivery-of-statistics-zimstat)).<br><br>The agency adopted a board charter setting the statutory board's roles, authority and ethical standards, and naming the professional independence and credibility of official statistics among what it safeguards ([charter](https://zimstat.co.zw/board-charter/)).<br><br>No amendment Bill, formal review or reform timetable is held: the agency's own account of the defect is the whole of the record.</details> | No change, the controlling Act called outdated by the agency it constitutes |

## Access to information legislation

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | The [media organic law requires public bodies to facilitate journalists' access to information](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/jo-francais/2023/F2023056.pdf); no general right-to-information statute is held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [organic law of 27 August 2023 governing media and information activity includes provisions requiring public bodies to facilitate journalists' access to information](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/jo-francais/2023/F2023056.pdf).<br><br>That is a press-access provision inside a media statute, not a general right of access. No right-to-information law, no request procedure, no refusal ground and no appeal route open to a citizen appears anywhere on this ledger, and no request, refusal or appeal under the media law's provisions is recorded either.</details> | No change, a first stated position with no earlier account behind it |
| Botswana | The [Access to Information Act was passed, its passage confirmed from the podium in November 2025](https://dailynews.gov.bw/news-detail/89031); no commencement notice is held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2024-07-26 — [the Act sets out the commission’s functions, its audit power and the identity-card regime](https://botswanalaws.com/consolidated-statutes/acts-on-notice/2024/access-to-information-act), with commencement on notice.<br><br>2025-11-11 — [the President confirmed passage in the State of the Nation Address](https://dailynews.gov.bw/news-detail/89031).<br><br>2025-10-17 — [the Digital Services Act subordinates itself to this Act and ties enterprise data inventories to its disclosure obligations](http://www.bocra.org.bw/sites/default/files/sites/default/files/documents/digital_services_ACT.pdf).<br><br>No commencement notice is on the record.</details> | Advanced, passed and not commenced |
| Burkina Faso | The access to public information law [was reported dormant in February 2025](https://burkina24.com/2025/02/11/droits-numeriques-au-burkina-faso-le-centre-national-de-presse-norbert-zongo-presente-son-rapport-2024/), and its number, date and text are not held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[A press-freedom body reported the access to public information law dormant](https://burkina24.com/2025/02/11/droits-numeriques-au-burkina-faso-le-centre-national-de-presse-norbert-zongo-presente-son-rapport-2024/), and no held source records any movement since. The law's number, date and text are not on the base, so what is recorded is an instrument known only by its inactivity.<br><br>Nothing else supplies the function. There is no open data portal, no dataset catalogue and no publication licence, reported under open data, and the data-protection authority's own statutory annual report has not been published since 2020. Transparency in this record runs through budget documents and ministry review meetings rather than through any right to ask.</details> | No change |
| Burundi | An access to information bill [was listed on the National Assembly's order paper for the session opened on 3 August 2026](https://lerenouveau.bi/assemblee-nationale-ouverture-de-la-session-parlementaire-ordinaire-daout-2026/), with no text published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-08-04 — [the bill was listed for examination in the session opened on 3 August 2026](https://lerenouveau.bi/assemblee-nationale-ouverture-de-la-session-parlementaire-ordinaire-daout-2026/). No text, tabling number, sponsoring ministry or timetable has been published, and none existed before this listing.<br><br>The session runs about three months under the constitution, which is the outer bound for passage in this sitting. Nothing else on this ledger touches access to information: there is no open data portal, no open data policy and no published dataset, so the bill would arrive into an absence rather than onto a practice.</details> | Advanced |
| Cameroon | The [e-government and access to information bill is still a preliminary draft](https://www.minpostel.gov.cm/images/Documentation/patnuc/textes_preparation_pour_consultation_publique/e-Government/230519_Loi%20EadmOpendata%20FIN_CLEAN.pdf); the [2018 transparency code stands](https://leconomie.info/finances-publiques-la-societe-civile-de-lest-mobilisee-pour-mieux-surveiller-le-budget-citoyen-2026/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The bill is [a preliminary draft on the right of access to and use of public information and the digital transformation of the administration, circulated in May 2023](https://www.minpostel.gov.cm/images/Documentation/patnuc/textes_preparation_pour_consultation_publique/e-Government/230519_Loi%20EadmOpendata%20FIN_CLEAN.pdf). Later reporting on its progress is in the base but is not citable, so the position stated here is older than the record.<br><br>The [transparency and good governance code promulgated on 11 July 2018 remains in force, requiring budget-information dissemination and civil-society involvement, and the finance ministry was still running first-contact sensitisation with civil society in April 2026](https://leconomie.info/finances-publiques-la-societe-civile-de-lest-mobilisee-pour-mieux-surveiller-le-budget-citoyen-2026/) - eight years after promulgation.</details> | No change |
| Cape Verde | Cabo Verde has a dedicated access-to-information statute: [Lei 10/X/2022 regulates access to and re-use of administrative documents](https://boe.incv.cv/Bulletins/Download/4230). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Lei 10/X/2022, gazetted in Boletim Oficial n.o 47 on 16 May 2022, regulates access to and re-use of administrative documents and administrative information held by the bodies and entities listed in its article 2](https://boe.incv.cv/Bulletins/Download/4230).<br><br>The base holds the gazette's own summary of the law and not its text: the full-issue capture stops before the law begins, and no per-act text was reachable. Nothing on the file states how the right is exercised in practice, what the response times are, or whether any refusal has been appealed.</details> | No change, a standing statute the base holds for the first time |
| Chad | [No freedom of information law has been adopted](https://upr-info.org/sites/default/files/country-document/2025-12/UNESCO_org_UPR45_TCD_E_Main.pdf), and the media regulator [suspended new online-newspaper applications](https://tchadinfos.com/2026/08/03/la-hama-suspend-la-creation-de-journaux-en-ligne-et-appelle-a-mettre-fin-au-desordre-informationnel/) in August 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>UNESCO's periodic review filing states at paragraph 13 that no freedom of information law has been adopted in Chad and at paragraph 19 recommends one be introduced to international standards, against a constitutional guarantee at article 28 of the 2018 constitution ([filing](https://upr-info.org/sites/default/files/country-document/2025-12/UNESCO_org_UPR45_TCD_E_Main.pdf)).<br><br>On 3 August 2026 two communiques from the media regulator suspended, until further notice, the examination of applications to create online newspapers, under its 2026-2028 strategic plan, and stated that offences on digital platforms fall outside its remit for now ([suspension](https://tchadinfos.com/2026/08/03/la-hama-suspend-la-creation-de-journaux-en-ligne-et-appelle-a-mettre-fin-au-desordre-informationnel/)).<br><br>The one movement the other way is publication rather than access: the Journal Officiel platform launched in May 2026 gives free online access to legal texts back to 1958 ([platform](https://www.undp.org/fr/chad/blog/un-clic-pour-acceder-plus-de-68-ans-de-memoire-institutionnelle-le-pari-numerique-du-tchad)).</details> | Regressed |
| Comoros | [No access-to-information law has been adopted](https://upr-info.org/sites/default/files/country-document/2026-01/UNESCO_org_UPR46_COM_E_Main.pdf), and none is recorded as drafted. <details><summary>Full record</summary>UNESCO's submission to the forty-sixth session of the universal periodic review states it plainly: [no access to information laws have been adopted in the Comoros](https://upr-info.org/sites/default/files/country-document/2026-01/UNESCO_org_UPR46_COM_E_Main.pdf). That is a dated, sourced absence rather than a gap in the record.<br><br>The [constitution guarantees freedom of information, communication and the press only within conditions established by law](https://upr-info.org/sites/default/files/country-document/2026-01/UNESCO_org_UPR46_COM_E_Main.pdf), and the instrument that law turned out to be regulates journalism: the [2021 communication code created the press and audiovisual council and made a press card a condition of practising](https://upr-info.org/sites/default/files/country-document/2026-01/UNESCO_org_UPR46_COM_E_Main.pdf). Neither creates a citizen right to obtain a government record.<br><br>Nothing on file records a draft, a commitment or a date.</details> | No change |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The [2013 access-to-information law's operational refresh was presented in July 2025](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2635603/culture/access-caidp-un-tournant-pour-la-transparence-et-lacces-a-linformation-en-cote-divoire) and nothing has been reported since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-07-25 - a programme was [presented at the Prime Minister's office as the operational refresh of the 2013 access-to-information law: a portal, a conformity audit tool, a helpline, a mobile application and a monitoring barometer](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2635603/culture/access-caidp-un-tournant-pour-la-transparence-et-lacces-a-linformation-en-cote-divoire).<br><br>No later source is on file in the year since. No request volumes, audit results or barometer output have been published, so whether any of the five components exists in service is unestablished, and the law's own text is not held.</details> | No change |
| Djibouti | [No access-to-information law exists](https://statemediamonitor.com/djibouti/); the 1992 law on freedom of communication is the only instrument on the field. <details><summary>Full record</summary>This is a searched absence rather than an unexamined one. A [country panel dated April 2026 records that the media and information regime is still governed solely by the law of 15 September 1992 on freedom of communication, never substantially reformed in more than three decades, with no dedicated access-to-information instrument](https://statemediamonitor.com/djibouti/).<br><br>The same panel names the communication regulator as the operative body and [places the country 167th of 180 on the 2026 press-freedom index](https://statemediamonitor.com/djibouti/).<br><br>The Digital Code's [own title on access to administrative documents](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/code-numerique.pdf) is the nearest statutory route, and it is a reuse provision rather than a right of access with a procedure.</details> | No change |
| DR Congo | A [bill setting access as the norm was declared admissible on 27 March 2026](https://actualite.cd/2026/03/28/assemblee-nationale-la-proposition-de-loi-sur-lacces-linformation-et-la-transparence-de); a [classification regime was presented two weeks earlier](https://actualite.cd/2026/03/20/rdc-vers-linstitutionnalisation-dun-regime-de-classification-et-de-protection-des). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-03-27 - the bill was [declared admissible and referred to a joint commission with ten days to report: it sets access as the norm and limitation the exception, applies to public services and some private actors, and requires a simple, fast and free request procedure, proactive publication, reasoned refusals and a right of appeal](https://actualite.cd/2026/03/28/assemblee-nationale-la-proposition-de-loi-sur-lacces-linformation-et-la-transparence-de). Four months past that ten-day deadline, no commission report, plenary date or adoption is held.<br><br>2026-03-13 - an information note to the Council of Ministers set out [four levels of classification with access limited to cleared agents, traceability of transmissions, reinforced archiving and a ban on sending sensitive documents over private messaging](https://actualite.cd/2026/03/20/rdc-vers-linstitutionnalisation-dun-regime-de-classification-et-de-protection-des), tied by its own account to the security situation in the east.</details> | Mixed, an access-to-information bill was declared admissible while a four-level classification regime was put to the Council of Ministers |
| Egypt | Two [freedom of information bills were submitted to parliament](https://manassa.news/en/news/33330) in August 2026; nothing is enacted. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-05 - the minister of state for information [told the prime minister that coordination was under way with ministries and authorities to issue the freedom of information law quickly](https://www.dailynewsegypt.com/2026/07/05/egypt-coordinates-to-issue-freedom-of-information-law-open-media-dialogue/).<br><br>2026-08-09 - a member's bill was [submitted to the House with its text carried in the reporting](https://www.masrawy.com/news/news_egypt/details/2026/8/9/3030353/).<br><br>2026-08-10 - a [syndicate-backed bill was submitted after securing 60 lawmakers' signatures; it would create an authority for the protection of freedom of information](https://manassa.news/en/news/33330).<br><br>Two bills and a ministerial statement of intent, with no statute in force, is the position after a decade of the same commitment.</details> | Advanced |
| Equatorial Guinea | The [Senate passed the country's first transparency and access-to-information bill](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/pleno_de_aprobacion_de_los_proyectos_de_ley_de_transparencia_y_zonas_economicas) unanimously in May 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Prime Minister [opened the debate before the Chamber of Deputies in February 2026](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/comparecencia_de_osa_nsue_ante_el_pleno_de_la_camara_de_los_diputados) on transparency, access to information and public finance.<br><br>The [Senate passed the transparency and finance-access bill unanimously in May](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/pleno_de_aprobacion_de_los_proyectos_de_ley_de_transparencia_y_zonas_economicas). Nothing yet records its promulgation, an implementing regulation, or a request procedure a citizen could use.</details> | Advanced |
| Eritrea | No freedom of information law has been adopted, a UN review body states plainly ([review contribution](https://upr-info.org/sites/default/files/country-document/2026-01/UNESCO_org_UPR46_ERI_E_Main.pdf)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>A formal contribution to Eritrea's universal periodic review states that no freedom of information law has been adopted, and that the constitutional guarantee of access to information sits in a 1997 constitution whose operative reality is the 1996 press proclamation ([review contribution](https://upr-info.org/sites/default/files/country-document/2026-01/UNESCO_org_UPR46_ERI_E_Main.pdf)).<br><br>The 2026 world press freedom index places Eritrea last of 180 countries and territories for the third consecutive year ([index](https://rsf.org/en/2026-rsf-index-press-freedom-25-year-low)).<br><br>The absence is legislative and practical at once: there is no statute, no request procedure and no appeal route, so nothing measures how much information is released.</details> | No change |
| Eswatini | No access to information law exists. The ICT ministry's own [report to Parliament](https://parliament.gov.sz/publications/parliament_reports/docs/ANNUAL%20REPORT%20ICT%202025.pdf) lists developing a Bill as a departmental action-plan item. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The ministry's annual performance report for 2024/25 lists 'Develop Access to Information Bill' among its departmental action-plan items; the Bill is not tabled and no text or timetable is held ([report](https://parliament.gov.sz/publications/parliament_reports/docs/ANNUAL%20REPORT%20ICT%202025.pdf)).<br><br>A regional media-freedom body met the ministry's principal secretary on 25 February 2026 and said it was on standby to support enactment; the ministry reaffirmed cooperation on media self-regulation and made no enactment commitment ([communique](https://misa.org/blog/misa-engages-eswatini-government-on-expression-and-media-freedom/)).<br><br>Where the ministry has legislated on information it has legislated on publishing rather than access: online media publishing guidelines are complete on its own account.<br><br>Without an access right, the data governance policy's sharing between institutions has no counterpart obliging disclosure to a citizen.</details> | No change, a Bill named as an action-plan item and not tabled |
| Ethiopia | The state development bank adopted a policy [classifying client credit information and four other categories as permanently non-disclosable](https://www.thereporterethiopia.com/52220/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The policy, reported in August 2026, exempts client credit information, legal case files, strategy papers, security protocols and third-party contracts from declassification where disclosure could cause substantial harm to the bank, its stakeholders or the national interest, and lets the bank decline requests that would require it to create records it does not hold ([disclosure policy](https://www.thereporterethiopia.com/52220/)).<br><br>No instrument number, gazette reference or appeal mechanism is stated, and the base holds no general access-to-information statute against which to read it.</details> | Regressed |
| Gambia | The Act is in force but [most public institutions have yet to appoint the information officers it requires](https://www.voicegambia.com/gpu-says-access-to-information-law-yet-to-be-fully-implemented/), and requests go unanswered. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Twenty-one-day statutory requests are going unanswered, on the press union's account of 7 August 2026 ([union account](https://www.voicegambia.com/gpu-says-access-to-information-law-yet-to-be-fully-implemented/)).<br><br>No government response, compliance count or enforcement action is on record, so a statute that exists is being reported as unimplemented by the constituency that uses it, and by nobody else.</details> | No change |
| Ghana | The information commission [fined 254 public bodies GHS 20,000 each](https://www.graphic.com.gh/news/general-news/upsa-37-military-hospital-among-254-public-institutions-fined-by-rti-commission-over-report-delays.html) in August 2026 for failing to file their mandatory annual reports. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The fines were imposed under section 77(1) of the 2019 Act after reminders published in the Gazette, and the sanction count is the commission's own ([fines](https://www.graphic.com.gh/news/general-news/upsa-37-military-hospital-among-254-public-institutions-fined-by-rti-commission-over-report-delays.html)).<br><br>No collection figure, appeal outcome or compliance rate is published, so whether the fines were paid is not established, and the enforcement action itself is the only measure of the regime the base holds.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea | The [online legal gazette](https://mediaguinee.com/2025/06/digitalisation-le-journal-officiel-passe-au-numerique-une-innovation-du-secretariat-general-du-gouvernemente/) is free and carries more than 1,100 journals and 6,000 texts, and its index stops in April 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The gazette has been online and free to access since at least June 2025 ([gazette](https://mediaguinee.com/2025/06/digitalisation-le-journal-officiel-passe-au-numerique-une-innovation-du-secretariat-general-du-gouvernemente/)).<br><br>An acquisition attempt in August 2026 found the index carrying issues only to April 2025, so a publication channel that exists has stopped being current, and no access-to-information statute, request procedure or appeal route is held beside it.</details> | No change |
| Guinea-Bissau | The access to information bill remains in development, against [platform blocking](https://mfwa.org/country-highlights/guinea-bissau-military-takeover-triggers-media-suspension-online-restrictions/) in November 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The bill sits on the ledger as in development and nothing in the window records it enacted.<br><br>What did move went the other way: after the November 2025 takeover [coup leaders ordered radio shutdowns and blocked Facebook, YouTube, TikTok and Instagram nationwide](https://mfwa.org/country-highlights/guinea-bissau-military-takeover-triggers-media-suspension-online-restrictions/), and the transitional council [passed a law against false online information](https://ang.gw/cnt-aprova-com-alteracoes-lei-contra-divulgacao-de-falsas-informacoes-nas-redes-sociais/) six months later.</details> | No change |
| Kenya | An [amendment bill would criminalise sharing classified information](https://nation.africa/kenya/news/new-bill-sharing-state-secrets-on-whatsapp-emails-could-land-you-in-jail-5516338) through personal messaging or email, on four classification tiers. <details><summary>Full record</summary>No sponsor, stage or timetable is on file ([bill](https://nation.africa/kenya/news/new-bill-sharing-state-secrets-on-whatsapp-emails-could-land-you-in-jail-5516338)).<br><br>It is the only access-to-information instrument in the base, and it narrows rather than widens: the record holds no compliance figure, request statistic or enforcement action under the existing regime against which to read it.</details> | Regressed |
| Lesotho | A 2021 capacity assessment found [no access-to-information legislation in force](https://pub.sbc4d.com/2022/lesotho_full_final.pdf), and nothing since records one being enacted. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The assessment carried out for the Millennium Challenge Corporation and the Lesotho Millennium Development Agency found no access-to-information statute in force in September 2021 ([assessment](https://pub.sbc4d.com/2022/lesotho_full_final.pdf)).<br><br>No bill, draft or commitment to one has appeared in the base in the five years since, so the absence is a searched one at that date and an unexamined one after it.</details> | No change |
| Liberia | The telecommunications regulator continues to refuse to publish what the gateway monitoring system earns ([report](https://smartnewsliberia.com/pressure-mounts-on-massaquoi-to-disclose-revenue/)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The telecommunications regulator's board chairman again declined to say what the international gateway monitoring system has earned, and the dispute has acquired a legal edge ([report](https://smartnewsliberia.com/pressure-mounts-on-massaquoi-to-disclose-revenue/)).</details> | No change |
| Libya | The [cabinet's draft bill](https://lawsociety.ly/legislation/%D9%85%D8%B4%D8%B1%D9%88%D8%B9-%D9%85%D8%B3%D9%88%D8%AF%D8%A9-%D9%82%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%88%D9%86-%D8%A8%D8%B4%D8%A3%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%86%D8%B8%D8%A7%D9%85-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%88%D8%B7%D9%86%D9%8A-%D9%84/) sets a request-and-appeal procedure and remains unenacted. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [draft law on the national information system](https://lawsociety.ly/legislation/%D9%85%D8%B4%D8%B1%D9%88%D8%B9-%D9%85%D8%B3%D9%88%D8%AF%D8%A9-%D9%82%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%88%D9%86-%D8%A8%D8%B4%D8%A3%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%86%D8%B8%D8%A7%D9%85-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%88%D8%B7%D9%86%D9%8A-%D9%84/) would give Libya a request-and-appeal route to public records. It has not been enacted.<br><br>What publication happens is voluntary: the [audit bureau published its 2024 annual report on 31 December 2025 after delay](https://www.audit.gov.ly/ar/2024-2/), with no right-to-information law compelling it. The [constitutional declaration](https://security-legislation.ly/ar/latest-laws/%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A5%D8%B9%D9%84%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AF%D8%B3%D8%AA%D9%88%D8%B1%D9%8A-%D9%84%D8%B3%D9%86%D8%A9-2011-%D9%85%D8%B9-%D8%AA%D8%B9%D8%AF%D9%8A%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%AA%D9%87/) guarantees expression and privacy and creates no access right.</details> | No change |
| Madagascar | The access to information bill was [left off the extraordinary session](https://www.rfi.fr/fr/afrique/20260827-madagascar-une-session-extraordinaire-de-l-assembl%C3%A9e-nationale-sans-le-projet-de-loi-sur-l-information-publique) in August 2026, eight months after being presented to Parliament. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A decree of 3 December 2025 presented an 88-article bill on access to public information to Parliament; its blank adoption, review and promulgation fields confirm it is not enacted ([decree](https://pub-7e0d3a35c1b949698a7ced344fa56252.r2.dev/textes/017-2025/PROJET_LAICP___PR_SENT_E_AU.PDF)).<br><br>The bill went to committee on 8 May 2026 with a plenary vote expected on 18 May ([committee](https://www.rfi.fr/fr/afrique/20260511-madagascar-l%C3%A9gislative-projet-loi-information-%C3%A0-caract%C3%A8re-public)).<br><br>An extraordinary session opened on 25 August 2026 with seven bills, the access bill again absent despite a committee undertaking to reinscribe it ([session](https://www.rfi.fr/fr/afrique/20260827-madagascar-une-session-extraordinaire-de-l-assembl%C3%A9e-nationale-sans-le-projet-de-loi-sur-l-information-publique)). No access to information law is in force.</details> | Regressed |
| Malawi | The [High Court ruled on 6 March 2026 that the Act reaches private companies performing government contracts](https://www.maraviexpress.com/access-to-information-act-applies-to-private-companies-undertaking-malawi-government-contracts-rules-the-high-court/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The statute's own text is now held: the [Access to Information Act, Act 13 of 2017, assented 10 February 2017, commenced 30 September 2020 and is overseen by the Human Rights Commission](https://malawilii.org/akn/mw/act/2017/13/eng@2017-02-16).<br><br>2025-10 - the commission's own implementation report for 2024-2025 [records 34 institutions trained and more than 36,000 requests processed, alongside a concept note proposing amendments for stronger enforcement](https://africabrief.substack.com/p/malawi-advances-information-access).<br><br>2026-03 - the [High Court held that the Act applies to private companies undertaking government contracts](https://www.maraviexpress.com/access-to-information-act-applies-to-private-companies-undertaking-malawi-government-contracts-rules-the-high-court/), which extends its reach past the public body it was drafted around. The ruling is reported rather than held as a judgment, and no appeal is on record.</details> | Advanced |
